- 2026-11-02 Entity leak fix (reviewing the profile-diff deploy above, Boss asked for a follow-up review since lag was "แทบจะไม่แลคละ"). A fresh Lag Report decoded cleanly and showed every metric much improved (ping p50 90->39ms/max 799->78ms, snapshot_age max 593->72ms, interpolation starvation max 1015->40.6ms, 0% of samples stalled >=200ms vs 9% before, 0 corrections) confirming the profile-diff fix worked. But it also showed the client reporting 37-42 "entities" while the worker's own live sim had only 5-6 fighters, and the client count never decreased across the whole 30s window. Code review confirmed: neither `server/rooms/netfox/service.gd`'s `_sync_nodes`/`_tick` nor `client/session/netfox_session.gd`'s `_accept_metadata` ever freed a replicated `Entity` (+ its `RemoteState` synchronizer) once its id dropped out of the live sim (monster died/despawned) - no `.erase()` call existed anywhere for either side's entity dict. Each leaked Entity keeps broadcasting (authority, 20Hz) or interpolating (client, every frame) a frozen position for the rest of the room/epoch, compounding with every enemy that has ever existed - worse the longer a session runs or the more content is packed into a stage (Boss: "อนาคต เราจะยัดเข้าไปมากกว่านี้"). Fixed by extracting the shared removal logic into `runtime/netfox/entity_prune.gd` (same pattern as `profile_diff.gd`), called from both sides. New `tests/test_entity_prune.gd` (9 checks, pure logic via fake entity stubs) plus live multi-process verification via `tools/netcode/migration/check_room.py` (solo/coop/epoch cases clean; one `diagnostics`-case flake traced via baseline A/B comparison under the same heavy system load to a pre-existing timing sensitivity unrelated to this change - baseline failed the same way). Full suite 2270 passed/170 failed, diffed line-by-line against the pre-change list: identical, zero new failures. Boss approved "แก้เลย"; build/deploy follows.
  DEPLOYED as `entity-prune-20261102-1` (commit `b06cec1`, Web build `cbca0c69a74a`). Built from an isolated `git worktree` checked out exactly at this commit (`/tmp/zorx-deploy-worktrees/...`, removed after) instead of the main working tree, because another session's uncommitted farm-sprite-art work (`client/farm_panel.gd`, `client/hub_world.gd`, `art/props/farm_crops/`, etc. - see the two farm entries above) was sitting in this repo's working directory at the time; that work was never touched, built, or deployed by this release. Web `wiyazard:30500/zorx-web:entity-prune-20261102-1@sha256:c3ab53b07bf82741b430000bc199b795e01b3c57472221a57b8c480781ce985b` on `game-test`, room-worker `wiyazard:30500/zorx-room-worker:entity-prune-20261102-1@sha256:0705bccf10a166d45d95208ad113f481c2021143cee5e9831ce83d4e8cff9c27` on `headless`'s `rooms` container; `profiles`/name-validator and Nakama again deliberately not rebuilt (no `server/names/` change). Rollouts SUCCESS (`game-test` 1/1, `headless` 2/2, 0 restarts), clean "name validator: ready" startup both containers, public `packs.json` confirms build/commit match exactly. Rollback refs + pre-rollout manifests saved to `build/k3s-remote/releases/entity-prune-20261102-1/`. `dirty: true` on the public manifest is a known cosmetic-only false positive from the metadata script's git-status check inside a worktree; the build's content hash matches this commit exactly, confirmed via direct file checks before building.

- 2026-11-02 **Login broken on entity-prune-20261102-1, fixed same session.** Boss reported "มีปัญหา login" with a title-screen screenshot showing "ยังไม่เชื่อมต่อ · ต้องต่ออินเทอร์เน็ตเพื่อเล่น" on Build `cbca0c69a74a` (the entity-prune deploy above). Root cause, confirmed directly (not guessed): `data/online.local.json` (gitignored, holds the real Nakama `client_key` + `enabled:true`, normally a standing local file generated once by `pipeline/online_client_config.py` against the private Secret bundle - see that script) was never copied into the isolated `git worktree` used to build that release, so `client/online/connection_config.gd`'s `load_default()` silently fell back to the checked-in `data/online.json` placeholder, which has `"enabled": false`. Confirmed by downloading the live core.pck and finding `data/online.local.json` absent, `data/online.json` present with `enabled:false` - exactly reproducing the screenshot. Unrelated to any of today's three netfox/combat fixes; `server/names`/Nakama/profiles were never touched.
  Fixed fast with a scoped patch instead of a full rebuild (new `tools/web/build_online_config_patch.py`, same proven pattern as the existing `build_lag_report_patch.py`/`build_gem_patch.py` family): downloaded the live `cbca0c69a74a` build's actual files from the public URL as a local baseline, swapped in only `data/online.local.json`, repacked `core.pck`, and verified via the patch script's own byte-for-byte assertion that every other file is unchanged (`unrelated_core_and_content_identical: true`, `core_delta_bytes: 183`). Verified the patched core directly (not just trusting the script) before and after pushing: decoded the PCK and confirmed `data/online.local.json` present with `enabled:true` and a real key, both in the local output and by re-downloading the live core.pck.wasm from the public URL post-deploy. DEPLOYED as Web build `689fd27c1ab3` over baseline `cbca0c69a74a`, image `wiyazard:30500/zorx-web:online-config-fix-20261102-1@sha256:14efcc1523fadc1c913e17cafb951ba1e75e87c93d0aeb5f79265f13a01bd951` on `game-test` only (room-worker/profiles/Nakama unaffected, not touched). Rollout SUCCESS, public `packs.json` and a direct core.pck re-download both confirm the fix live.
  Process note for next time a worktree-isolated build is needed: copy BOTH `build/names-binaries/*.zip` (Godot Linux binary cache, avoids a slow/fragile fresh download) AND `data/online.local.json` into the fresh worktree before exporting - this was missed for `data/online.local.json` specifically because it is a silently-optional fallback (`FileAccess.file_exists` check, no error raised) rather than a hard build failure, so a missing-file CI gate would not have caught it either; needs an explicit pre-export checklist step, not just the binary-cache fix from earlier today.

- 2026-11-04 **"VFX ยังไม่อลังการ" / game-feel pass, three parts, deployed together as `combat-feel-20261104-1`.** Boss felt combat VFX weren't impactful enough. Scoped down twice on Boss's correction: first dropped basic weapon-swing tuning entirely ("ไม่ต้องไปทำพวกตี") once Boss pointed out those moves' classic per-weapon special/ultimate variants are dead code under `skill_build.enabled()` (confirmed in `logic/game_sim.gd`: it forces `input.special=false; input.ultimate=false` for every skill_build player) - so the only moves worth tuning are the skill-tree's 20 skills + their gems, and the monster/goblin telegraph warning.
  1. **Skill + skill-gem impact** (combat-designer agent). Found `"skill_hit"`/`"skill_dot"` sim events only flashed the target and showed a damage number - zero camera shake/punch/flash, zero hit-stop, despite both already existing and used by ordinary weapon hits. Added a 3-tier impact system (`data/skill_system/integration.json`'s new `impact` block: tier 1/2/3 per skill, hit-stop `[0,4,6,10]` ticks on target / `[0,0,2,4]` on caster, +2 on crit capped at 12 - for reference a weapon light swing is 4 and heavy is 8), element-colored sparks per skill (`skill_colors` in `data/combat/vfx.json`), and a mark for each effect gem at the moment of impact (not just an ambient icon) - also found and fixed a bug where "leech" gem never rendered anything at all. `leech`/`thorns`/`regen` (no bespoke `status_vfx` asset) fall back to generic buff/debuff marks as an explicit placeholder. Camera kick fires once per frame even on an 8-target AoE, and only when the local player cast or took the hit (so allies' skills never shake your screen in a group fight - flagged for Boss to confirm). New `tests/test_skill_impact.gd` (26 checks).
  2. **Monster attack telegraph warning** (combat-designer agent, run after #1 finished to avoid both editing `client/stage_view.gd` at once). The wind-up warning was a flat yellow `BoxMesh`, same color regardless of move, no sense of timing. New `logic/combat.gd::telegraph_shape()` reuses the game's own targeting to build a lane (rush/lunge, including diagonals; swings offset to the real hit box) or a circle (AoE slams, the mage's hex, aimed at the actual target or a fallback) matching each move's real shape, drawn red by a new `client/vfx/attack_telegraph.gdshader` with a gauge that fills as the wind-up progresses and reaches the far end the frame the hit lands. F3 debug hitboxes are now active-attacks-only; the wind-up marker is a separate reused quad. 6 new checks in `tests/test_combat.gd`.
  3. **Goblin stage dressing** (level-designer agent, ran in parallel with #1/#2 since it touches none of the same files). The "rough stone box" Boss meant turned out to be bare collision volumes showing through - the stage builder only put spheres *inside* the collider boxes, invisible, so the empty box itself was all that rendered. New `tools/blender/build_goblin_base_dressing.py` builds a wooden crate/mossy stone block/log wall sized to fill each collider; `tools/maps/build_goblin_base.gd` swaps them in via MultiMesh. The old grass (read as black specks) replaced with `verdant_reach`'s grass/fern meshes via `tools/maps/goblin_terrain.gd`, clumped at obstacle feet and the palisade, clear of the path/spawns/exits/rest point/campfire. +1.4% draw calls, no loader/format/client code touched.

  All three verified independently by their agents (zero new test failures each, windowed-Godot visual checks with screenshots) and then re-verified together on the merged tree by the PO: full suite **2303 passed / 170 failed**, same 170 pre-existing failures (profile/M1-M4/camp areas, none combat-related) and same 10 pre-existing SCRIPT ERRORs both before and after - re-ran twice (once pre-commit, once post-commit) with identical numbers. One cleanup needed first: an earlier, separately-killed combat-designer run (basic-swing hitstop/burst tuning, abandoned when Boss said to skip basic attacks) had left uncommitted edits sitting in `data/combat/vfx.json`/`data/combat/weapons.json` that the skill-impact agent correctly left alone rather than guess about; the PO hand-separated the diff (every hunk checked against each agent's own file list) and reverted only the abandoned swing-tuning portion before committing, confirmed by a clean 2-hunk diff on `vfx.json` and a fully-reverted `weapons.json`. Committed as three focused commits (`eaeea0f` skill impact, `a70cbfd` telegraph, `06d77bf` goblin dressing).
  DEPLOYED as `combat-feel-20261104-1` (Web build `589967df2fc8`, core `cfceb7bac3ed`). The web pipeline's own embedded test gate (`pipeline/build_web.py`) requires exactly zero failing tests project-wide, which this project has never had (170 pre-existing, unrelated, baseline-known) - built with `--skip-tests --max-file-mb 0` (the second flag matching `build_web_container.sh --build-web`'s own established invocation, since `stages.*.pck.wasm` has always been ~20 MiB, over the artifact-host-oriented 15 MiB default) and relied on the PO's own standalone `run_all.gd` regression-diff instead, per this project's established gating convention. Web `wiyazard:30500/zorx-web:combat-feel-20261104-1@sha256:d2993d8a8c38493db57951c1eb430928510c3a0deb72ff4adb377703a8ff44e4` on `game-test`, room-worker `wiyazard:30500/zorx-room-worker:combat-feel-20261104-1@sha256:0bbf6b8ac534e7d3e13a74854a10273db46672764c17e7780342262fb2db9504` on `headless`'s `rooms` container (rebuilt because `logic/combat.gd`/`logic/skills/system.gd`/`logic/skills/runtime.gd` are server-authoritative); `profiles`/name-validator untouched (no `server/names/` change). Rollouts SUCCESS (`game-test` 1/1, `headless` 2/2, 0 restarts), clean "name validator: ready" startup both containers, public `packs.json` confirms build `589967df2fc8`/commit `06d77bf` exactly. Verified `data/online.local.json enabled:true` at all three checkpoints (fresh export, pushed Docker image via direct extraction, and the live re-downloaded `core.cfceb7bac3ed.pck.wasm` from `https://zorxplay.daiyooo.com` post-deploy). Rollback manifests saved to `build/k3s-remote/releases/combat-feel-20261104-1/`.
  **Open decisions left for Boss** (shipped with placeholder values, not blocking this deploy per standing deploy authorization): (1) are the skill hit-stop tiers right, especially tier-3 skills at 10 ticks vs. a weapon heavy swing's 8; (2) is it right that an ally's skill never shakes your camera in group play; (3) `leech`/`thorns`/`regen` gem visuals and the `ice_lance`/`lightning_strike`/`ground_slam` base skill assets still read as weak even with the new impact layer - likely needs character-artist; (4) keep or drop the mage hex's new red-circle-then-purple-circle two-step warning; (5) shorten the archer volley's warning lane to the target instead of its full 13 m range; (6) should the "!" warning icon above a telegraphing monster's head turn red to match the new ground warning (still yellow). Also noted but out of scope for this pass: recurring `Parameter "material" is null` renderer errors during live combat, unrelated to any of these three changes (seen identically before and after) - worth a look from tech-artist.
